                       APPENDIX F

SUMMARY OF CERTAIN PROVISIONS OF THE FINANCING AGREEMENT
[This page intentionally left blank]
                                             APPENDIX F

         SUMMARY OF CERTAIN PROVISIONS OF THE FINANCING AGREEMENT

        The following is a summary, which does not purport to be complete, comprehensive or definitive,
of certain provisions of the Financing Agreement, which is qualified in its entirety by reference to the
Financing Agreement.

Financing Structure

                (a)     The Lender agrees, subject to the terms and conditions of the Financing
        Agreement and the Lender Commitment, to make a loan to the Issuer in an amount equal to the
        outstanding principal amount of the Mortgage Loans as of the Closing Date, which will be
        evidenced by the Mortgage Notes, as amended. The Issuer agrees, subject to the terms and
        provisions of the Financing Agreement, to issue the Bonds and to make the proceeds thereof
        available to purchase the GNMA Securities. The Issuer agrees, subject to the terms and
        conditions of the Financing Agreement, to use the proceeds of the Mortgage Loans to refinance
        the Prior Bonds and pay certain costs in connection therewith.

                 (b)     Upon issuance of the GNMA Securities by the Lender, the Trustee, on behalf of
        the Issuer, has been authorized and directed under the Indenture to purchase the GNMA
        Securities from the Lender with funds in the Acquisition Fund and with respect to the payment of
        accrued and unpaid interest on the GNMA Securities with funds in the Bond Fund in accordance
        with the Financing Agreement and the Indenture, at a price specified in the Financing Agreement.
        The Trustee agrees to acquire the GNMA Securities as set forth in the Financing Agreement and
        in the Indenture.

               (c)      Pursuant to the GNMA Mortgage-Backed Securities Guide, the Lender agrees to
        make all payments on the GNMA Securities in accordance with their terms, provided such
        agreement shall not be construed as the Lender’s guaranty.

                (d)      Nothing in the Financing Agreement shall be construed to impose a duty on the
        Trustee to purchase, or on the Lender to issue, GNMA Securities in a principal amount in excess
        of the outstanding balance of the Mortgage Notes as of the Closing Date.

                (e)      The Issuer agrees to remit to the Lender any amounts which the Trustee is
        required to pay the Lender, but for which sufficient funds to make such payments are not
        available under the Indenture.

Delivery of the GNMA Securities

        The Issuer and the Lender agree under the Financing Agreement to use their best efforts to deliver
to the Trustee the GNMA Securities as soon as practicable after the Closing Date. The Financing
Agreement provides that the Issuer and the Lender expect to deliver the GNMA Securities to the Trustee
on or before April 4, 2013. The Financing Agreement further states that neither the Issuer nor the Lender
has actual knowledge of a material fact that causes it to believe that the GNMA Securities will not be
delivered to the Trustee on or before such date.




                                                   F-1
Sufficiency of Funds

        The Issuer agrees that if the Issuer should pay any costs relating to the acquisition of the GNMA
Securities other than from the proceeds of the Bonds, the Issuer shall not be entitled to any reimbursement
therefor from the Lender, the Trustee or the Bondholders.

Operation of the Project

        The Financing Agreement provides that the Issuer shall operate or cause the Project to be
operated as an eligible project under Sections 241 and 242 of the National Housing Act.

Events of Default; Remedies

         Upon receipt by a Responsible Officer of the Trustee of notice of a violation by the Issuer of, or
default by the Issuer under, any of the provisions of the Financing Agreement, the Trustee shall give
written notice thereof to the Issuer by certified mail, postage prepaid, return-receipt requested. If a
violation or default by the Issuer of any of the provisions of the Financing Agreement is not corrected to
the reasonable satisfaction of the Trustee within 30 days after the date such notice is received, or if the
violation or default (other than a payment default) cannot be corrected within such period, within such
longer period as may be necessary, in the reasonable opinion of the Trustee, to correct such violation,
provided that the Issuer has commenced and is diligently pursuing appropriate action to correct such
violation and there will be no material adverse effect on the rights of the Trustee, the Lender or the
Bondholders under the Financing Agreement, any of the FHA Loan Documents or the Indenture as a
result of such extension, without further notice, the Trustee may declare a default under the Financing
Agreement effective on the date of such declaration of default, and, upon such default, the Issuer, the
Lender or the Trustee may apply to any state or federal court having jurisdiction (a) for specific
performance of the Financing Agreement or for an injunction against any violation of the Financing
Agreement, since the injury to the Issuer, the Lender and the Trustee arising from a default under any of
the terms of the Financing Agreement would be irreparable, and the amount of damage would be difficult
to ascertain, or (b) for other relief in law or equity which may be appropriate. A default under the
Financing Agreement shall not constitute an Event of Default under the Indenture, the FHA Loan
Documents or the documents relating to the GNMA Securities. Notwithstanding the occurrence of any
event of default under the Financing Agreement, the Trustee shall continue to purchase the GNMA
Securities from the Lender and to make disbursements pursuant to the Financing Agreement, and the
funds on deposit in the Acquisition Fund and the Bond Fund shall remain available for such purpose.

GMNA/FHA Loan Documents and Regulations Control; FHA Requirements

        (a)      To the extent that there is any conflict, inconsistency or ambiguity between or among the
Financing Agreement and (i) any applicable FHA mortgage insurance, or other applicable FHA or
GNMA statutory, regulatory, administrative requirements; (ii) any of the FHA Loan Documents which
have been or are required by FHA and/or the Lender to be executed by the Issuer, FHA and/or the Lender
in connection with the subject transaction or (iii) any of the GNMA Documents which have been or are
required by GNMA to be executed by the Issuer, FHA, GNMA and/or the Lender in connection with the
subject transaction, said FHA mortgage insurance and other applicable FHA and GNMA statutory,
regulatory and administrative requirements and said FHA Loan Documents and GNMA Documents will
be deemed to be controlling and any such ambiguity or inconsistency will be resolved in favor of, and
pursuant to, the FHA mortgage insurance, and other applicable FHA and GNMA statutory, regulatory or
administrative requirements and the terms of the FHA Loan Documents and GNMA Documents, as
applicable. For purposes of the Financing Agreement, the reference to FHA’s statutory, regulatory and
administrative requirements shall be deemed to include, but shall not be limited to, any statutory,



                                                   F-2
regulatory or administrative requirements pertaining to Sections 241 and 242 of the National Housing
Act, as may be applicable. The parties to the Financing Agreement agree to amend the instrument as may
be necessary or required by FHA, GMNA or the Lender to conform the instrument to the above-cited
requirements and FHA Loan Documents and GNMA Documents. In addition, it is understood and agreed
that any default under the Financing Agreement shall not constitute a default under the FHA Loan
Documents or the GNMA Documents; and further, that nothing in the Financing Agreement contained
shall be construed to limit or affect the Lender’s rights under the FHA Loan Documents or the GNMA
Documents.

       (b)     No amendment to the Financing Agreement shall conflict with the provisions of the
National Housing Act, any applicable HUD regulations, related administrative requirements, the FHA
Loan Documents, any applicable GNMA regulations, related administrative requirements and the GNMA
Documents.

        (c)     The Lender will hold escrow funds required under the Mortgage Loans as required by
FHA and/or GNMA outside the terms of the Indenture and the Financing Agreement, and any Project
funds held by the Lender will not be deposited in any of the funds or accounts created by the Financing
Agreement or under the control of the Trustee.

        (d)     Nothing contained in the Financing Agreement shall inhibit or impair the right of the
Lender to require or agree to any amendment, charge or modification of the FHA Loan Documents or the
GNMA Documents for the purpose of curing any ambiguity, or of curing, correcting or supplementing
any defective or inconsistent provision contained in the Financing Agreement, or in regard to matters or
questions arising under said FHA Loan Documents or the GNMA Documents so long as any such
amendment, change or modification shall not adversely affect the payment terms of the Bonds.

         (e)    The Financing Agreement shall not be construed to restrict or adversely affect the duties
and obligations of the Lender under the contract of mortgage insurance between FHA and the Lender with
respect to the Mortgage Loans.

        (f)     The Financing Agreement shall not entitle the Trustee, or any of the Bondholders, to
assert any lien against the Project, the Mortgage Loans proceeds, any reserves or deposits required by
HUD in connection with the Mortgage Loans transaction, or the revenues, receipts, rents or deposits or
other income of the Project.

         (g)     Proceeds from any condemnation award or from the payment of a claim under any hazard
insurance policy relating to the Project will not be payable to the Trustee, but will be payable to the
Lender to the extent proceeds of any casualty insurance, condemnation award or other amounts received
following a partial or total destruction or condemnation of the Project are not applied to the repair or
restoration of the Project, and the Bonds will be subject to redemption in whole or in part, prior to their
stated maturity date, in said event from payments the Trustee receives on the GNMA Securities
representing and subsequent to a prepayment of the Mortgage Notes and the Mortgages from such
proceeds or claims payment.

        (h)    In the event of any conflict between the provisions of this Section 9.10 and the provisions
contained in any other Section of the Financing Agreement, the provisions of this Section 9.10 shall
govern and be controlling in all respects.

              APPENDIX G

FORM OF CONTINUING DISCLOSURE AGREEMENT
[This page intentionally left blank]
                       CONTINUING DISCLOSURE AGREEMENT
         This Continuing Disclosure Agreement (the “Disclosure Agreement”), dated March 21, 2013, is
by and between MAIMONIDES MEDICAL CENTER (the “Issuer”) and U.S. BANK NATIONAL
ASSOCIATION, as Trustee (defined herein) on behalf of the holders of the Issuer’s $100,535,000
aggregate principal amount of GNMA Collateralized Taxable Revenue Bonds, Series 2013 (the “Bonds”).
The Bonds are being issued by the Issuer and sold to the underwriter (defined below) as described in the
official statement, dated February 27, 2013 (the “Official Statement”). The Bonds are being issued
pursuant to a Trust Indenture dated as of March 1, 2013 (the “Indenture”) between the Issuer and U.S.
Bank National Association, as trustee (the “Trustee”). The proceeds of the Bonds are being loaned to the
Issuer pursuant to a Financing Agreement dated as of March 1, 2013 (the “Financing Agreement”) among
the Issuer, the Trustee and Prudential Huntoon Paige Associates, LLC, a Delaware limited liability
company (the “Lender”). The Issuer and Trustee covenant and agree as follows:

         Section 1. Purpose of this Disclosure Agreement. This Disclosure Agreement is being
executed and delivered by the Issuer and the Trustee for the benefit of the holders of the Bonds. The
Issuer and the Trustee acknowledge that neither the Issuer nor the Trustee shall have any monetary
liability to any Person, including any holder of the Bonds or Beneficial Owner, with respect to any
reports, notices or disclosures required to be provided pursuant to this Disclosure Agreement.

        Section 2. Definitions. In addition to the definitions set forth in the Indenture, which apply to
any capitalized term used in this Disclosure Agreement unless otherwise defined in this Section, the
following capitalized terms shall have the following meanings:

        “Annual Report” shall mean any Annual Report provided by the Issuer pursuant to, and as
described in, Sections 3 and 4 of this Disclosure Agreement.

         “Beneficial Owner” shall mean any Person which (a) has the power, directly or indirectly, to vote
or consent with respect to, or to dispose of ownership of, any Bonds (including Persons holding Bonds
through nominees, depositories or other intermediaries), or (b) is treated as the owner of any Bonds for
federal income tax purposes.

        “DAC” shall mean the Digital Assurance Certification LLC, or such other Person as the Issuer
shall designate in writing to the Trustee from time to time.

       “Disclosure Representative” shall mean the Chief Financial Officer of the Issuer or his or her
designee, or such other Person as the Issuer shall designate in writing to the Trustee from time to time.

           “Listed Events” shall mean any of the events listed in Section 5(a) of this Disclosure Agreement.

        “Quarterly Report” shall mean any Quarterly Report provided by the Issuer pursuant to, and as
described in, Sections 3 and 4 of this Disclosure Agreement.

           “Underwriter” means Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith Incorporated and its successors and
assigns.

         Section 3. Provision of Reports. (a) The Issuer will provide to DAC for electronic posting on
DAC’s website, not later than 135 days following the end of the Issuer’s fiscal year, commencing with the
fiscal year ending in December 31, 2013, an Annual Report which is consistent with the requirements set
forth in Section 4(a) below. The Issuer will also provide to DAC for electronic posting on DAC’s
website, not later than 60 days following the end of the Issuer’s first three fiscal quarters, commencing
with the fiscal quarter ending June 30, 2013, a Quarterly Report which is consistent with the requirements

                                                     G-1
set forth in Section 4(b) below. In each case, the Annual Report and/or Quarterly Report may be
submitted as a single document or as separate documents comprising a package and may cross reference
other information, provided that the audited financial statements for the prior fiscal year of the Issuer or
the unaudited financial statements for the fiscal quarter of the Issuer, as the case may be, may be
submitted separately from the balance of the Annual Report and/or Quarterly Report. The Issuer
covenants and agrees to provide the Trustee with an electronic copy of every Annual Report and
Quarterly Report at the same time that it provides such information to DAC.

        (b)    If the Trustee has not received an electronic copy of the Annual Report and/or Quarterly
Report from the Issuer by the applicable date specified in subsection (a), the Trustee shall (i) notify the
Disclosure Representative that the Issuer is not in compliance with subsection (a), and (ii) upon
confirmation of such non-compliance shall provide DAC with an electronic notice to such effect for
posting on DAC’s website.

        (c)     Upon the timely receipt of each Annual Report and/or Quarterly Report, the Issuer shall
request from DAC a written confirmation of receipt of the Annual Report and/or Quarterly Report,
including the date it was received by DAC.

        Section 4. Content of Reports. (a) The Issuer’s Annual Report will contain or incorporate by
reference the following:

                 (i)     the audited financial statements of the Issuer for its prior fiscal year, prepared in
        accordance with generally accepted accounting principles as promulgated from time to time by
        the Financial Accounting Standards Board (or any successor entity), and modified as necessary to
        comply with HUD reporting. If the Issuer’s audited financial statements are not available by the
        time the Annual Report is required to be filed, the Annual Report will contain unaudited financial
        statements in a format similar to the financial statements contained in the Official Statement (i.e.,
        including a balance sheet, statement of operations, changes in net assets and cash flow for the
        fiscal year), and the audited financial statements will be filed in the same manner as the Annual
        Report when they become available; and

                 (ii)     certain annual financial information and operating data relating to it for its prior
        fiscal year, in scope, content and presentation similar to the information set forth in the Official
        Statement (i.e., including aggregate inpatient discharges, patient days, average length of stay,
        emergency room visits, outpatient visits and outpatient procedures; and discharges by major
        payor source for the fiscal year), with an explanation of any significant trends or changes.

        (b)     The Issuer’s Quarterly Report will contain or incorporate by reference the following:

                 (i)      the Quarterly Report will contain unaudited financial statements of the Issuer for
        its prior fiscal quarter in a format similar to the unaudited financial statements contained in the
        Official Statement (i.e., including a balance sheet, statement of operations, changes in net asses
        and cash flow as of the end of the fiscal quarter); and

                (ii)     interim financial information and operating data relating to it for its prior fiscal
        quarter, in scope, content and presentation similar to the information set forth in the Official
        Statement (i.e., including aggregate inpatient discharges, patient days, average length of stay,
        emergency room visits, outpatient visits and outpatient procedures; and discharges by major
        payor source for the fiscal quarter), with an explanation of any significant trends or changes.

        Section 5. Reporting of Significant Events. (a) The Issuer will give, or cause to be given,
notice of the occurrence of any of the following events with respect to the Bonds:


                                                    G-2
                         (i)      principal and interest payment delinquencies;

                         (ii)     non payment related defaults, if material;

                         (iii)    [Reserved];

                         (iv)     unscheduled draws on credit enhancements reflecting financial
                                  difficulties;

                         (v)      substitution of credit or liquidity providers or their failure to perform;

                         (vi)     [Reserved];

                         (vii)    modifications to rights of Bondholders, if material;

                         (viii)   Bond calls, if material and tender offers;

                         (ix)     defeasances;

                         (x)      release, substitution or sale of property securing repayment of the Bonds;

                         (xi)     rating changes;

                         (xii)    bankruptcy, insolvency, receivership of similar event of the Issuer;

                         (xiii)   the consummation of a merger, consolidation or acquisition involving the
                                  Issuer, or the sale of all or substantially all of the assets of the Issuer,
                                  other than in the ordinary course of business, the entry into a definitive
                                  agreement to undertake such an action or the termination of a definitive
                                  agreement relating to any such actions, other than pursuant to its terms, if
                                  material; and

                         (xiv)    appointment of a successor or additional Bond trustee, or the change of
                                  name of a Bond trustee, if material.

        (b)      Upon obtaining knowledge of the occurrence of a Listed Event for which a materiality
standard exists, the Issuer agrees to promptly determine if such event is considered material for purposes
of applicable federal securities laws.

        (c)      If the Issuer has determined that knowledge of the occurrence of a Listed Event would be
material under applicable federal securities laws, or if the Listed Event does not involve a materiality
standard, the Issuer will (i) promptly notify the Trustee in writing, and (ii) electronically file with DAC a
notice of the occurrence of such Listed Event for posting on DAC’s website.

         (d)    If in response to a request under subsection (b), the Issuer determines that the Listed
Event would not be material under applicable federal securities laws, the Issuer will so notify the Trustee
in writing and (except with respect to Section 5(a)(ii) above) provide a concurring opinion of counsel to
the Trustee.

        Section 6. Amendment; Waiver. Notwithstanding any other provision of this Disclosure
Agreement, the Issuer and the Trustee may amend this Disclosure Agreement and any provision of this
Disclosure Agreement may be waived, provided that the following conditions are satisfied:


                                                     G-3
                (a)     If the amendment or waiver relates to the provisions described under paragraph
        (a) under “Provision of Reports”, “Contents of Reports” or paragraph (a) under “Reporting of
        Significant Events”, it may only be made in connection with a change in circumstances that arises
        from a change in legal requirements, change in law or change in the identity, nature or status of
        an obligated Person with respect to the Bonds or the type of business conducted; and

                (b)      The amendment or waiver either (i) is approved by the Holders of the Bonds in
        the same manner as provided in the Indenture for amendments to the Indenture with the consent
        of Holders or (ii) does not, in the opinion of Transaction Counsel, materially impair the interests
        of the Holders or Beneficial Borrowers of the Bonds.

        Notwithstanding the foregoing, no amendment or waiver shall be required if there is a change in
accounting principles as required under generally accepted accounting principles.

        In the event of any amendment or waiver of a provision of this Disclosure Agreement, the Issuer
covenants and agrees to (i) describe such amendment in the next Annual Report and (ii) as applicable, a
narrative explanation of the reason for the amendment or waiver and its impact on the type (or, in the case
of a change of accounting principles, on the presentation) of financial information or operating data being
presented by the Issuer. In addition, if the amendment relates to the accounting principles to be followed
in preparing financial statements, the Annual Report for the year in which the change is made should
present a comparison (in narrative form and also, if feasible, in quantitative form) between the financial
statements as prepared on the basis of the new accounting principles and those prepared on the basis of
the former accounting principles. Notwithstanding the foregoing, a separate narrative explanation shall
not be required if described in the relevant financial statements.

       The Issuer will provide a copy of any amendment to this Disclosure Agreement to the Trustee
promptly following its execution.

         Section 7. Remedies. In the event of a failure by either the Issuer or the Trustee to comply with
any provision of this Disclosure Agreement, the non-defaulting party, may, and, at the request of the
Underwriter or the Holders of at least 25% aggregate principal amount of Outstanding Bonds, shall, take
such actions by mandamus or other suit or proceeding at law or in equity as may be necessary and
appropriate against the other party and any of its officers, agents and employees, in order to cause the
defaulting party to comply with its obligations under this Disclosure Agreement; provided, however, that
the sole and exclusive remedy for breach of this Disclosure Agreement shall be an action to compel
specific performance of the obligations of such defaulting party hereunder and no person or entity shall be
entitled to recover monetary damages hereunder under any circumstances.

        A default under this Disclosure Agreement shall not be deemed an Event of Default under the
Indenture or the Financing Agreement.

         Section 8. Beneficiaries. This Disclosure Agreement will inure solely to the benefit of the
Issuer, the Trustee, the Underwriter and Holders from time to time of the Bonds and will create no rights
in any other Person or entity.

        Section 9. GNMA/FHA Documents and Regulations Control. Notwithstanding any provision
of this Disclosure Agreement to the contrary, the parties hereto acknowledge and agree that all of their
respective rights and powers under this Agreement are subordinate and subject to the liens of the
Mortgages created by the Issuer in favor of the Lender under the FHA Loan Documents, and to all of the
terms and provisions of the Mortgages, and any and all other FHA Loan Documents and GNMA
Documents executed by the Issuer, FHA, GNMA and/or the Lender, as required by FHA, GNMA or the
Lender in connection with the Mortgage Loans.


                                                   G-4
         Section 10. Additional Information. Nothing in this Disclosure Agreement shall be deemed to
prevent the Issuer from disseminating any other information, using the means of dissemination set forth in
this Disclosure Agreement or any other means of communication, or including any other information in
any Annual Report, Quarterly Report or notice of occurrence of a Listed Event, in addition to that which
is required by this Disclosure Agreement. If the Issuer chooses to include any information in any Annual
Report, Quarterly Report or notice of occurrence of a Listed Event, in addition to that which is
specifically required by this Disclosure Agreement, the Issuer shall have no obligation under this
Disclosure Agreement to update such information or include it in any future Annual Report or notice of
occurrence of a Listed Event.

        Section 11. Duties, Immunities and Liabilities of the Trustee. For purposes of this Disclosure
Agreement, the Trustee shall have only such duties as are specifically set forth herein, and the Issuer
agrees to indemnify and save the Trustee and its officers, directors, employees and agents, harmless
against any loss, expense and liabilities which they may incur arising out of or in the exercise or
performance of their rights, obligations, powers and duties hereunder, including the costs and expenses
(including reasonable attorneys’ fees) of defending against any claim of liability, but excluding liabilities
due to the Trustee’s negligence or willful misconduct. The obligations of the Issuer under this Section
shall survive the termination of this Disclosure Agreement, the resignation or removal of the Trustee
hereunder and the payment of the Bonds.

         The Trustee shall not be deemed to be acting in any fiduciary capacity for the Issuer or the
holders of the Series 2013 Bonds or any other party. The Trustee shall have no duty or obligation (i) with
respect to the form or content of any disclosure or Listed Events Notices provided by the Issuer pursuant
to the terms of this Disclosure Agreement, or (ii) to review or verify any information contained in the
Annual Reports or the Quarterly Reports or Listed Event Notices or any other information, disclosures or
notices provided to it by the Issuer. The Trustee shall have no responsibility for the Issuer’s failure to
electronically provide to DAC any notices of Listed Events required by this Disclosure Agreement or
duty to determine the materiality thereof. The Trustee shall have no duty to determine or liability for
failing to determine whether the Issuer has complied with this Disclosure Agreement and it may
conclusively rely upon certifications of the Issuer with respect to its obligations hereunder. In no event
shall the Trustee have any liability to the holders of the Bond or any other party for any monetary
damages or financial liability of any kind whatsoever related to or arising from the breach of this
Disclosure Agreement.

        In the event that the obligations of the Trustee hereunder terminate, the Issuer will either appoint
a successor for the benefit of the Series 2013 Bondholders.

        No provision of this Disclosure Agreement shall require or be construed to require the Issuer or
the Trustee to interpret or provide an opinion concerning any information disclosed hereunder.

        The Annual Report may contain such disclaimer language as the Issuer may deem appropriate.

         The Issuer hereby agrees to compensate the Trustee for the services provided pursuant to this
Disclosure Agreement, in an amount to be agreed upon, and to reimburse the Trustee upon its request for
all reasonable expenses, disbursements and advances incurred by it hereunder (including any reasonable
compensation and expenses of counsel) except any such expense, disbursement or advance that may be
attributable to its negligence or willful misconduct.

        The Trustee may consult with counsel of its choice and the written advice of such counsel or any
opinion of counsel shall be full and complete authorization and protection in respect of any action taken,
suffered or omitted by it hereunder in good faith and in reliance thereon, it being understood that for
purposes of this provision, that such counsel may be counsel to the Issuer.

        No provision of this Disclosure Agreement shall require the Trustee to expend or risk its own
funds or otherwise incur any financial liability in the performance of any of its duties hereunder, or in the
exercise of any of its rights of powers.

Ladies and Gentlemen:

        We have acted as transaction counsel to Maimonides Medical Center (the “Issuer”), a New York
not-for-profit corporation organized under the New York State not-for-profit corporation law and a
charitable organization within the meaning of Section 501(c)(3) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986, in
connection with the issuance of $100,535,000 in aggregate principal amount of its GNMA Collateralized
Taxable Revenue Bonds, Series 2013, dated the date hereof (the “Bonds”). The Bonds are issued by the
Issuer under and pursuant to that certain Trust Indenture dated as of March 1, 2013 (the “Indenture”)
between the Issuer and U.S. Bank National Association, as trustee (the “Trustee”).

       The proceeds from the sale of the Bonds will be used by the Issuer to purchase fully modified
mortgage-backed securities which will be guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and interest by the
Government National Mortgage Association from Prudential Huntoon Paige Associates, LLC (the
“Lender”) pursuant to that certain Financing Agreement dated as of March 1, 2013 (the “Financing
Agreement”) among the Issuer, the Trustee and the Lender.

        In our capacity as transaction counsel to the Issuer, we have examined, among other things,
proceedings of the Issuer authorizing, among other things, the execution and delivery of the Indenture, the
Financing Agreement, and the issuance of the Bonds, a specimen Bond, a certificate of the Trustee
regarding the authentication of the Bonds, executed counterparts of the above-referenced documents, the
executed opinion of Arent Fox LLP, New York, New York, counsel to the Issuer, dated this date, and
such other documents, showings and related matters as we have deemed necessary in order to render this
opinion.

       Based on the foregoing, but subject to the assumptions, qualifications and limitations set forth
below, we are of the opinion that:

         1.     The Indenture has been duly authorized by the Issuer, has been duly executed and
delivered by authorized officers of the Issuer and, assuming due authorization, execution and delivery
thereof by the Trustee, constitutes a valid and binding instrument of the Issuer, enforceable against the
Issuer in accordance with its terms.



                                                     H-1
March 21, 2013
Page 2

        2.      The Bonds have been duly authorized by the Issuer, have been duly executed by
authorized officers of the Issuer, authenticated by the Trustee and validly issued by the Issuer and
constitute the valid and binding limited obligations of the Issuer payable solely from payments and
prepayments and from other amounts payable under the under the Indenture and the Bonds are
enforceable in accordance with their terms and are entitled to the benefit and security of the Indenture.

         3.      The Financing Agreement has been duly authorized by the Issuer, has been executed and
delivered by authorized officers of the Issuer and, assuming due authorization, execution and delivery
thereof by the other parties thereto, constitutes a valid and binding instrument of the Issuer enforceable
against the Issuer in accordance with its terms.

         The opinions set forth above are subject to the following assumptions, qualifications and
limitations:

        (a)       We have assumed without any independent investigation (i) the genuineness of all
        signatures on documents examined by us and the authority of the persons to sign such documents
        (other than the genuineness of the signature of the officers of the Issuer on the Indenture, the
        Financing Agreement and the Bonds, and the authority of such officers to sign such documents),
        (ii) the authenticity of all documents submitted to us as originals and the conformity to authentic
        original documents of all documents submitted to us as certified, conformed, facsimile or
        photostatic copies, and (iii) the correctness of public files, records and certificates of, or furnished
        by, governmental or regulatory agencies or authorities.

        (b)      We have assumed (without expressing any opinion thereon), to the extent the obligations
        of the Issuer or its performance thereof may be dependent on such matters, that: (i) each party to
        the Indenture and the Financing Agreement (other than the Issuer) is duly organized, validly
        existing and in good standing under the laws of its jurisdiction of incorporation, and (ii) any party
        seeking to enforce its rights against the Issuer under the Indenture or the Financing Agreement is
        qualified to do business in every state necessary in order for it to realize the practical benefits of
        enforcing its rights under such document. We also have assumed (i) the due authorization,
        execution and delivery of the Indenture and the Financing Agreement by all parties (other than
        the Issuer), (ii) that each party to the Indenture and the Financing Agreement (other than the
        Issuer) has the requisite organizational power and authority to perform its obligations under such
        document, and (iii) the validity and binding effect of the Indenture and the Financing Agreement
        with regard to such parties (other than the Issuer).

        (c)     Our opinions in paragraphs 2 and 3 are subject, as to enforceability, to the effect of any
        applicable bankruptcy, insolvency, reorganization, moratorium or similar laws affecting creditors’
        rights generally from time to time in effect, and to the effect of general principles of equity,
        including without limitation those related to equitable subordination, matters of charitable trust or
        public policy, concepts of materiality, reasonableness, good faith and fair dealing (regardless of
        whether considered in a proceeding in equity or at law) and the possible unavailability of the
        remedy of specific performance or payment of attorney fees.

        (d)     We offer no opinion as to whether any authorizations, consents, approvals, licenses,
        permits, filings or registrations may be required (or whether any fees may be required to be paid
        in connection therewith) in connection with any legal or other proceedings for enforcement of the
        Indenture and the Financing Agreement.

       (e)     We express no opinion as to the applicability of laws relating to fraudulent transfer,
       fraudulent obligations or preferential transfer, or any laws governing the distribution of assets of
       the Issuer including without limitation, Section 548 of Chapter 11 of the United States Code.

       (f)   We express no opinion as to any provision of any document purporting to provide for the
       payment of interest on interest.

       (g)    We express no opinion as to the inclusion of interest on the Bonds in the gross income of
